Abstract: Machine learning models are routinely deployed on a wide range of computing hardware. Although such hardware is typically expected to produce identical results, differences in its design can lead to small numerical variations during inference. In this work, we show that these variations can be exploited to create backdoors in machine learning models. The core idea is to shape the model's decision function such that it yields different predictions for the same input when executed on different hardware. This effect is achieved by locally moving the decision boundary close to a target input and then refining numerical deviations to flip the prediction on selected hardware. We empirically demonstrate that these hardware-triggered backdoors can be created reliably across common GPU accelerators. Our findings reveal a novel attack vector affecting the use of third-party models, and we investigate different defenses to counter this threat.

</details>


### [237] [LoRIF: Low-Rank Influence Functions for Scalable Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21929)
*Shuangqi Li,Hieu Le,Jingyi Xu,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRIF：利用梯度低秩结构解决训练数据归因的存储和计算瓶颈，实现20倍存储减少和查询加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的训练数据归因方法（如TRAK、LoGRA）面临两个主要瓶颈：1）存储和加载所有训练样本的投影梯度导致I/O延迟问题；2）形成D×D逆Hessian近似需要O(D²)内存。这两个瓶颈都随投影维度D增加而恶化，但提高D对归因质量至关重要，形成了质量-可扩展性权衡。

Method: LoRIF利用梯度的低秩结构解决两个瓶颈：1）存储投影梯度的秩-c因子而非完整矩阵，将每层每样本的存储和I/O从O(D)降至O(c√D)；2）使用截断SVD结合Woodbury恒等式在r维子空间中近似Hessian项，将内存从O(D²)降至O(Dr)。

Result: 在0.1B到70B参数的模型和数百万样本的数据集上，LoRIF相比LoGRA实现高达20倍的存储减少和查询时间加速，同时匹配或超过其归因质量。

Conclusion: LoRIF通过利用梯度低秩结构解决了梯度基训练数据归因的存储和计算瓶颈，使其在前沿规模上变得实用。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) identifies which training examples most influenced a model's prediction. The best-performing TDA methods exploits gradients to define an influence function. To overcome the scalability challenge arising from gradient computation, the most popular strategy is random projection (e.g., TRAK, LoGRA). However, this still faces two bottlenecks when scaling to large training sets and high-quality attribution: \emph{(i)} storing and loading projected per-example gradients for all $N$ training examples, where query latency is dominated by I/O; and \emph{(ii)} forming the $D \times D$ inverse Hessian approximation, which costs $O(D^2)$ memory. Both bottlenecks scale with the projection dimension $D$, yet increasing $D$ is necessary for attribution quality -- creating a quality-scalability tradeoff. We introduce \textbf{LoRIF (Low-Rank Influence Functions)}, which exploits low-rank structures of gradient to address both bottlenecks. First, we store rank-$c$ factors of the projected per-example gradients rather than full matrices, reducing storage and query-time I/O from $O(D)$ to $O(c\sqrt{D})$ per layer per sample. Second, we use truncated SVD with the Woodbury identity to approximate the Hessian term in an $r$-dimensional subspace, reducing memory from $O(D^2)$ to $O(Dr)$. On models from 0.1B to 70B parameters trained on datasets with millions of examples, LoRIF achieves up to 20$\times$ storage reduction and query-time speedup compared to LoGRA, while matching or exceeding its attribution quality. LoRIF makes gradient-based TDA practical at frontier scale.

</details>


### [238] [Entropy-Based Dimension-Free Convergence and Loss-Adaptive Schedules for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21943)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Ziqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出信息论方法实现维度无关的扩散模型收敛分析，无需几何假设，并基于KL散度重构提出轻量级损失自适应调度方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型收敛分析通常与维度线性相关，且需要内在维度假设或几何限制。需要开发不依赖几何假设的维度无关收敛分析方法

Method: 采用信息论方法，基于KL散度重构，提出损失自适应调度(LAS)方案，仅依赖训练损失进行反向SDE离散化，无需后训练重计算

Result: 在温和假设下，目标分布与生成分布间的KL散度界为O(H²/K)，其中H为香农熵，K为采样步数。LAS在实验中优于常见启发式调度

Conclusion: 信息论方法实现了维度无关的扩散模型收敛分析，LAS作为轻量级调度方案提升了采样质量，无需几何假设或后训练重计算

Abstract: Diffusion generative models synthesize samples by discretizing reverse-time dynamics driven by a learned score (or denoiser). Existing convergence analyses of diffusion models typically scale at least linearly with the ambient dimension, and sharper rates often depend on intrinsic-dimension assumptions or other geometric restrictions on the target distribution. We develop an alternative, information-theoretic approach to dimension-free convergence that avoids any geometric assumptions. Under mild assumptions on the target distribution, we bound KL divergence between the target and generated distributions by $O(H^2/K)$ (up to endpoint factors), where $H$ is the Shannon entropy and $K$ is the number of sampling steps. Moreover, using a reformulation of the KL divergence, we propose a Loss-Adaptive Schedule (LAS) for efficient discretization of reverse SDE which is lightweight and relies only on the training loss, requiring no post-training heavy computation. Empirically, LAS improves sampling quality over common heuristic schedules.

</details>


### [239] [Clarity: The Flexibility-Interpretability Trade-Off in Sparsity-aware Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21944)
*Konstantinos P. Panousis,Diego Marcos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种评估视觉语言模型中概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）可解释性的框架，重点关注不同建模决策如何影响学习到的概念表示，并引入了"清晰度"这一衡量指标来量化下游性能与概念表示稀疏性、精确性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）的广泛应用引发了对模型可解释性的担忧，这些模型通常被视为黑箱，对其决策过程的研究有限。尽管存在多种事后和事前可解释性方法，但对学习到的表示进行系统客观评估仍然不足，特别是对于被认为能"诱导可解释性"的稀疏性感知方法。该研究旨在填补这一空白，重点关注概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）。

Method: 研究引入了"清晰度"这一衡量指标，用于捕捉下游性能与概念表示稀疏性和精确性之间的相互作用。提出了一个使用具有真实概念标注数据集的评估框架。考虑了基于VLM和属性预测器的CBMs，以及三种不同的稀疏性诱导策略：基于每个样本的ℓ₁、ℓ₀和伯努利公式。通过实验分析不同建模决策对概念表示的影响。

Result: 实验揭示了灵活性与可解释性之间的关键权衡，表明即使在可比较的性能水平下，给定方法也可能表现出显著不同的行为。不同稀疏性诱导策略在概念表示的质量和可解释性方面产生不同的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估概念瓶颈模型的可解释性提供了一个系统框架，强调了在追求模型性能时需要平衡可解释性考虑。提出的"清晰度"指标和评估方法有助于更客观地比较不同可解释性方法，代码将在发表后公开。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) across fields has amplified concerns about model interpretability. Distressingly, these models are often treated as black-boxes, with limited or non-existent investigation of their decision making process. Despite numerous post- and ante-hoc interepretability methods, systematic and objective evaluation of the learned representations remains limited, particularly for sparsity-aware methods that are increasingly considered to "induce interpretability". In this work, we focus on Concept Bottleneck Models and investigate how different modeling decisions affect the emerging representations. We introduce the notion of clarity, a measure, capturing the interplay between the downstream performance and the sparsity and precision of the concept representation, while proposing an interpretability assessment framework using datasets with ground truth concept annotations. We consider both VLM- and attribute predictor-based CBMs, and three different sparsity-inducing strategies: per example $\ell_1, \ell_0$ and Bernoulli-based formulations. Our experiments reveal a critical trade-off between flexibility and interpretability, under which a given method can exhibit markedly different behaviors even at comparable performance levels. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [240] [Embracing Aleatoric Uncertainty in Medical Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21950)
*Linxiao Gong,Yang Liu,Lianlong Sun,Yulai Bi,Jing Liu,Xiaoguang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AUM框架，通过建模单模态的偶然不确定性来处理医学多模态学习中的缺失模态问题，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多模态学习面临临床实践中普遍存在的模态缺失问题。现有方法假设模态贡献相等且缺失模式随机，忽略了医学数据采集中的固有不确定性。

Method: 提出偶然不确定性建模(AUM)：1) 将每个单模态表示建模为多元高斯分布以捕捉偶然不确定性；2) 在二分患者-模态图中开发动态消息传递机制，使用不确定性感知聚合机制自适应聚合信息；3) 自然容纳缺失模态，同时动态强调可用模态中更可靠的信息。

Result: AUM框架在MIMIC-IV死亡率预测上AUC-ROC提升2.26%，在eICU上提升2.17%，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模单模态偶然不确定性，AUM框架能够有效处理医学多模态学习中的模态缺失问题，实现更可靠的信息聚合和表示生成。

Abstract: Medical multimodal learning faces significant challenges with missing modalities prevalent in clinical practice. Existing approaches assume equal contribution of modality and random missing patterns, neglecting inherent uncertainty in medical data acquisition. In this regard, we propose the Aleatoric Uncertainty Modeling (AUM) that explicitly quantifies unimodal aleatoric uncertainty to address missing modalities. Specifically, AUM models each unimodal representation as a multivariate Gaussian distribution to capture aleatoric uncertainty and enable principled modality reliability quantification. To adaptively aggregate captured information, we develop a dynamic message-passing mechanism within a bipartite patient-modality graph using uncertainty-aware aggregation mechanism. Through this process, missing modalities are naturally accommodated, while more reliable information from available modalities is dynamically emphasized to guide representation generation. Our AUM framework achieves an improvement of 2.26% AUC-ROC on MIMIC-IV mortality prediction and 2.17% gain on eICU, outperforming existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [241] [Investigation into using stochastic embedding representations for evaluating the trustworthiness of the Fréchet Inception Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21979)
*Ciaran Bench,Vivek Desai,Carlijn Roozemond,Ruben van Engen,Spencer A. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了FID在医学图像评估中的局限性，通过蒙特卡洛dropout计算FID的预测方差，评估其在处理分布外数据时的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID作为合成图像质量评估指标，基于ImageNet预训练的InceptionV3模型，在医学图像应用中存在局限性。研究者希望量化FID在捕捉图像特征差异时的有效性，特别是在处理分布外数据时的可靠性。

Method: 使用蒙特卡洛dropout方法计算FID的预测方差，同时计算特征嵌入模型潜在表示的预测方差。通过ImageNet1K验证集（以不同强度增强）和其他外部数据集作为测试输入，评估这些方差与输入数据相对于训练数据分布外程度的关联性。

Result: 预测方差的大小与测试输入相对于训练数据的分布外程度存在不同程度的关联。这种关联性为评估FID作为医学图像质量指标的可信度提供了洞察。

Conclusion: FID在医学图像评估中的有效性有限，预测方差可以作为评估FID可信度的指标。该方法为理解基于自然图像预训练模型在医学图像应用中的局限性提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Feature embeddings acquired from pretrained models are widely used in medical applications of deep learning to assess the characteristics of datasets; e.g. to determine the quality of synthetic, generated medical images. The Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is one popular synthetic image quality metric that relies on the assumption that the characteristic features of the data can be detected and encoded by an InceptionV3 model pretrained on ImageNet1K (natural images). While it is widely known that this makes it less effective for applications involving medical images, the extent to which the metric fails to capture meaningful differences in image characteristics is not obviously known. Here, we use Monte Carlo dropout to compute the predictive variance in the FID as well as a supplemental estimate of the predictive variance in the feature embedding model's latent representations. We show that the magnitudes of the predictive variances considered exhibit varying degrees of correlation with the extent to which test inputs (ImageNet1K validation set augmented at various strengths, and other external datasets) are out-of-distribution relative to its training data, providing some insight into the effectiveness of their use as indicators of the trustworthiness of the FID.

</details>


### [242] [PowerGenie: Analytically-Guided Evolutionary Discovery of Superior Reconfigurable Power Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21984)
*Jian Gao,Yiwei Zou,Abhishek Pradhan,Wenhao Huang,Yumin Su,Kaiyuan Yang,Xuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerGenie：一个用于大规模自动发现高性能可重构功率转换器的框架，通过自动化分析框架和进化微调方法，发现了比最佳训练拓扑FoM高23%的新型8模式可重构转换器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电路拓扑发现依赖人类专家，现有AI方法要么从预定义模板中选择，要么在有限规模下生成新拓扑且缺乏严格验证，大规模性能驱动发现尚未充分探索。

Method: 1) 自动化分析框架：无需元件尺寸确定或SPICE仿真即可确定转换器功能和理论性能极限；2) 进化微调方法：通过适应度选择和唯一性验证，使生成模型与其训练分布共同进化。

Result: 相比现有方法，PowerGenie实现了更高的语法有效性、功能有效性、新颖率和品质因数(FoM)。发现的新型8模式可重构转换器比最佳训练拓扑FoM高23%，SPICE仿真确认在8种模式下平均绝对效率提升10%，单模式下最高提升17%。

Conclusion: PowerGenie框架成功实现了大规模高性能可重构功率转换器的自动发现，克服了现有方法的模式崩溃和过拟合问题，为电路设计自动化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Discovering superior circuit topologies requires navigating an exponentially large design space-a challenge traditionally reserved for human experts. Existing AI methods either select from predefined templates or generate novel topologies at a limited scale without rigorous verification, leaving large-scale performance-driven discovery underexplored. We present PowerGenie, a framework for automated discovery of higher-performance reconfigurable power converters at scale. PowerGenie introduces: (1) an automated analytical framework that determines converter functionality and theoretical performance limits without component sizing or SPICE simulation, and (2) an evolutionary finetuning method that co-evolves a generative model with its training distribution through fitness selection and uniqueness verification. Unlike existing methods that suffer from mode collapse and overfitting, our approach achieves higher syntax validity, function validity, novelty rate, and figure-of-merit (FoM). PowerGenie discovers a novel 8-mode reconfigurable converter with 23% higher FoM than the best training topology. SPICE simulations confirm average absolute efficiency gains of 10% across 8 modes and up to 17% at a single mode. Code is available at https://github.com/xz-group/PowerGenie.

</details>


### [243] [Elign: Equivariant Diffusion Model Alignment from Foundational Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21985)
*Yunyang Li,Lin Huang,Luojia Xia,Wenhe Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Elign是一个后训练框架，通过预训练的机器学习力场提供物理信号，并使用强化学习将物理引导转移到训练阶段，从而在保持快速推理的同时生成具有更低DFT能量和更高稳定性的分子构象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的E(3)-等变扩散模型往往复制半经验训练数据的偏差，而非捕获高保真哈密顿量的平衡分布。虽然基于物理的引导可以纠正这一问题，但面临两个计算瓶颈：昂贵的量子化学评估（如DFT）以及需要在每个采样步骤重复此类查询。

Method: 提出Elign后训练框架：1) 用预训练的机器学习力场（MLFF）替代昂贵的DFT评估提供物理信号；2) 将物理引导转移到训练阶段，通过将反向扩散表述为强化学习问题，引入FED-GRPO（力-能量解耦组相对策略优化）来微调去噪策略，包括基于势能的能量奖励和基于力的稳定性奖励，并进行独立优化和组归一化。

Result: 实验表明，Elign生成的构象具有更低的金标准DFT能量和力，同时提高了稳定性。关键的是，由于在生成过程中不需要能量评估，推理速度与无引导采样一样快。

Conclusion: Elign通过将昂贵的物理评估转移到训练阶段并利用预训练的MLFF，成功解决了基于物理引导的计算瓶颈问题，实现了在保持快速推理的同时生成更符合物理规律的分子构象。

Abstract: Generative models for 3D molecular conformations must respect Euclidean symmetries and concentrate probability mass on thermodynamically favorable, mechanically stable structures. However, E(3)-equivariant diffusion models often reproduce biases from semi-empirical training data rather than capturing the equilibrium distribution of a high-fidelity Hamiltonian. While physics-based guidance can correct this, it faces two computational bottlenecks: expensive quantum-chemical evaluations (e.g., DFT) and the need to repeat such queries at every sampling step. We present Elign, a post-training framework that amortizes both costs. First, we replace expensive DFT evaluations with a faster, pretrained foundational machine-learning force field (MLFF) to provide physical signals. Second, we eliminate repeated run-time queries by shifting physical steering to the training phase. To achieve the second amortization, we formulate reverse diffusion as a reinforcement learning problem and introduce Force--Energy Disentangled Group Relative Policy Optimization (FED-GRPO) to fine-tune the denoising policy. FED-GRPO includes a potential-based energy reward and a force-based stability reward, which are optimized and group-normalized independently. Experiments show that Elign generates conformations with lower gold-standard DFT energies and forces, while improving stability. Crucially, inference remains as fast as unguided sampling, since no energy evaluations are required during generation.

</details>


### [244] [Generalized Information Gathering Under Dynamics Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21988)
*Fernando Palafox,Jingqi Li,Jesse Milzman,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，将信息收集成本与具体建模选择解耦，基于Massey定向信息推导通用信息收集成本，证明互信息成本是其特例，并建立互信息成本与线性化贝叶斯估计中信息增益的明确联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动信息收集方法为特定建模选择（动力学模型、信念更新过程、观测模型、规划器）推导定制成本，缺乏统一框架。需要将信息收集成本与具体建模选择解耦，提供更通用的理论框架。

Method: 提出统一框架，通过显式暴露参数、信念和控制之间的因果依赖关系，解耦信息收集成本与建模选择。基于Massey定向信息推导通用信息收集成本，仅假设马尔可夫动力学和加性噪声。证明互信息成本是该通用成本的特例，并建立互信息成本与线性化贝叶斯估计中信息增益的理论联系。

Result: 1. 推导出通用的信息收集成本框架，适用于各种建模选择；2. 证明现有文献中的互信息成本是所提通用成本的特例；3. 建立了互信息成本与线性化贝叶斯估计中信息增益的明确理论联系；4. 通过线性、非线性和多智能体系统的实验验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架成功地将信息收集成本与具体建模选择解耦，为主动学习提供了更通用的理论基础。该框架不仅包含现有方法作为特例，还建立了互信息成本与信息增益的理论联系，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: An agent operating in an unknown dynamical system must learn its dynamics from observations. Active information gathering accelerates this learning, but existing methods derive bespoke costs for specific modeling choices: dynamics models, belief update procedures, observation models, and planners. We present a unifying framework that decouples these choices from the information-gathering cost by explicitly exposing the causal dependencies between parameters, beliefs, and controls. Using this framework, we derive a general information-gathering cost based on Massey's directed information that assumes only Markov dynamics with additive noise and is otherwise agnostic to modeling choices. We prove that the mutual information cost used in existing literature is a special case of our cost. Then, we leverage our framework to establish an explicit connection between the mutual information cost and information gain in linearized Bayesian estimation, thereby providing theoretical justification for mutual information-based active learning approaches. Finally, we illustrate the practical utility of our framework through experiments spanning linear, nonlinear, and multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [245] [Negatives-Dominant Contrastive Learning for Generalization in Imbalanced Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21999)
*Meng Cao,Jiexi Liu,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于不平衡领域泛化（IDG）的负主导对比学习方法，通过增强决策边界分离和强制领域间后验一致性来解决领域和标签偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不平衡领域泛化（IDG）需要同时处理领域偏移和标签偏移，这在异构长尾分布下尤为重要。然而，由于技术复杂性和理论基础的缺乏，该领域尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出负主导对比学习（NDCL）方法：1）通过强调负样本作为对比学习的主要信号来增强类间决策边界分离；2）使用重加权交叉熵策略促进类内紧凑性；3）通过预测中心对齐策略强制领域间后验一致性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的严格实验验证了NDCL方法的有效性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析建立了IDG的泛化界，并提出了直接引导决策边界的NDCL方法，有效解决了不平衡领域泛化中的挑战。

Abstract: Imbalanced Domain Generalization (IDG) focuses on mitigating both domain and label shifts, both of which fundamentally shape the model's decision boundaries, particularly under heterogeneous long-tailed distributions across domains. Despite its practical significance, it remains underexplored, primarily due to the technical complexity of handling their entanglement and the paucity of theoretical foundations. In this paper, we begin by theoretically establishing the generalization bound for IDG, highlighting the role of posterior discrepancy and decision margin. This bound motivates us to focus on directly steering decision boundaries, marking a clear departure from existing methods. Subsequently, we technically propose a novel Negative-Dominant Contrastive Learning (NDCL) for IDG to enhance discriminability while enforce posterior consistency across domains. Specifically, inter-class decision-boundary separation is enhanced by placing greater emphasis on negatives as the primary signal in our contrastive learning, naturally amplifying gradient signals for minority classes to avoid the decision boundary being biased toward majority classes. Meanwhile, intra-class compactness is encouraged through a re-weighted cross-entropy strategy, and posterior consistency across domains is enforced through a prediction-central alignment strategy. Finally, rigorous yet challenging experiments on benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our NDCL. The code is available at https://github.com/Alrash/NDCL.

</details>


### [246] [Exploring Diverse Generation Paths via Inference-time Stiefel Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22010)
*Dongxuan Zhu,Ly Tran Ho Khanh,Andy Yat-Ming Cheung,Man-Chung Yue,Viet Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STARS是一种无需训练、在推理时干预的方法，通过Stiefel流形优化激活导向，提升语言模型生成多样性，避免模式崩溃


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型倾向于生成高概率输出，导致生成路径同质化且容易发生模式崩溃。基于采样的策略虽然注入随机性，但难以保证多个并发生成运行之间的多样性

Method: STARS在推理时收集并发生成运行的隐藏激活，在Stiefel流形上联合优化多个加性导向方向。该方法最大化导向激活的几何体积，Stiefel流形诱导导向干预的正交性。为降低延迟，设计了轻量级一步更新和封闭形式步长

Result: 在测试用例生成和科学发现基准测试中，STARS始终优于标准采样方法，在不牺牲质量性能的情况下实现更大的多样性

Conclusion: STARS将激活导向转化为探索引擎，通过Stiefel流形优化显式促进并发生成运行的激活向量发散，隐式促进生成轨迹发散，有效解决了语言模型生成多样性不足的问题

Abstract: Language models often default to a narrow set of high-probability outputs, leaving their generation paths homogeneous and prone to mode collapse. Sampling-based strategies inject randomness but still struggle to guarantee diversity across multiple concurrent generation runs. We address this limitation by introducing STARS ($\textbf{St}$iefel-based $\textbf{A}$ctivation Steering for Diverse $\textbf{R}$ea$\textbf{S}$oning), a training-free, inference-time intervention method that transforms activation steering into an exploration engine. At each token, STARS collects the hidden activations of concurrent generation runs and optimizes multiple additive steering directions jointly on the Stiefel manifold. STARS maximizes the geometric volume of the steered activations, while the Stiefel manifold induces orthogonality of the steering interventions. This formulation explicitly promotes divergent activation vectors of concurrent generation runs, and implicitly promotes divergent generation trajectories. This manifold optimization formulation can be solved using a Riemannian gradient descent algorithm with convergence guarantees, but this algorithm is too time-consuming for real-time inference. To guarantee low latency, we further design a lightweight one-step update with an aggressive, closed-form stepsize. For test case generation and scientific discovery benchmarks, STARS consistently outperforms standard sampling methods, achieving greater diversity without sacrificing qualitative performance.

</details>


### [247] [Putting a Face to Forgetting: Continual Learning meets Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22012)
*Sergi Masip,Gido M. van de Ven,Javier Ferrando,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种机制性框架，从特征编码的几何变换角度解释灾难性遗忘，通过可处理模型形式化分析，并展示了在Vision Transformer上的应用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习研究主要从性能或最后一层表示层面衡量灾难性遗忘，忽视了底层机制。本文旨在从特征编码的几何变换角度深入理解遗忘机制。

Method: 引入机制性框架，将灾难性遗忘解释为个体特征编码的几何变换；通过可处理模型形式化分析；使用Crosscoders分析实际模型；在Vision Transformer上进行序列CIFAR-10训练的案例研究。

Result: 形式化分析识别了最佳和最差情况；实验验证了形式化分析并强调了深度的负面影响；案例研究展示了框架在实际模型分析中的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作为持续学习提供了新的、以特征为中心的词汇和理论框架，从特征编码的几何变换角度深入理解灾难性遗忘机制。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting in continual learning is often measured at the performance or last-layer representation level, overlooking the underlying mechanisms. We introduce a mechanistic framework that offers a geometric interpretation of catastrophic forgetting as the result of transformations to the encoding of individual features. These transformations can lead to forgetting by reducing the allocated capacity of features (worse representation) and disrupting their readout by downstream computations. Analysis of a tractable model formalizes this view, allowing us to identify best- and worst-case scenarios. Through experiments on this model, we empirically test our formal analysis and highlight the detrimental effect of depth. Finally, we demonstrate how our framework can be used in the analysis of practical models through the use of Crosscoders. We present a case study of a Vision Transformer trained on sequential CIFAR-10. Our work provides a new, feature-centric vocabulary for continual learning.

</details>


### [248] [TBDFiltering: Sample-Efficient Tree-Based Data Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22016)
*Robert Istvan Busa-Fekete,Julian Zimmert,Anne Xiangyi Zheng,Claudio Gentile,Andras Gyorgy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于文本嵌入的层次聚类方法，通过自适应选择文档让LLM评估来估计聚类质量，证明该方法查询高效且优于基于分类器的过滤方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大型语言模型选择高质量、多样化的训练数据面临挑战，缺乏廉价可靠的质量指标。现有方法要么查询LLM评估文档质量（不可扩展），要么使用基于稀疏质量信号的分类器（效果有限）。

Method: 提出基于文本嵌入的层次聚类方法：1) 对文档进行层次聚类；2) 自适应选择文档让LLM评估以估计聚类质量；3) 利用聚类结构减少查询数量。方法假设层次聚类中存在子树，其中每个叶簇足够"纯净"（主要包含好文档或坏文档）。

Result: 理论证明：在假设条件下，该方法能以高概率正确预测每个文档的质量，查询的文档数量与最小（几乎）纯净叶簇子树的大小成正比，无需预先知道该子树。实验研究表明，该方法优于其他基于分类器的过滤方法。

Conclusion: 提出的层次聚类方法为大规模文档质量评估提供了查询高效的解决方案，通过利用文档嵌入的语义相似性和聚类结构，显著减少了需要LLM评估的文档数量，同时保持高质量预测。

Abstract: The quality of machine learning models depends heavily on their training data. Selecting high-quality, diverse training sets for large language models (LLMs) is a difficult task, due to the lack of cheap and reliable quality metrics. While querying existing LLMs for document quality is common, this is not scalable to the large number (billions) of documents used in training. Instead, practitioners often use classifiers trained on sparse quality signals. In this paper, we propose a text-embedding-based hierarchical clustering approach that adaptively selects the documents to be evaluated by the LLM to estimate cluster quality. We prove that our method is query efficient: under the assumption that the hierarchical clustering contains a subtree such that each leaf cluster in the tree is pure enough (i.e., it mostly contains either only good or only bad documents), with high probability, the method can correctly predict the quality of each document after querying a small number of documents. The number of such documents is proportional to the size of the smallest subtree with (almost) pure leaves, without the algorithm knowing this subtree in advance. Furthermore, in a comprehensive experimental study, we demonstrate the benefits of our algorithm compared to other classifier-based filtering methods.

</details>


### [249] [Visual-Guided Key-Token Regularization for Multimodal Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22020)
*Chengyi Cai,Zesheng Ye,Peike Li,Bo Han,Jianzhong Qi,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ViKeR方法，通过视觉引导的关键令牌正则化解决MLLM遗忘学习问题，优先处理关键令牌并利用视觉线索提升遗忘效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM遗忘学习方法主要借鉴LLM方法，存在两个问题：1) 对所有答案令牌统一处理，忽视其在遗忘过程中的重要性差异；2) 仅关注语言模态，忽略视觉线索对关键令牌的指示作用

Method: 提出视觉引导的关键令牌正则化(ViKeR)：1) 利用无关视觉输入预测理想的遗忘后令牌级分布；2) 使用这些分布正则化遗忘过程，优先处理关键令牌；3) 通过信息熵定义遗忘中的关键令牌；4) 通过令牌级梯度重加权机制放大关键令牌的更新

Result: 在MLLMU和CLEAR基准测试中，ViKeR方法有效执行遗忘学习，同时减轻遗忘效应并保持回答连贯性

Conclusion: ViKeR通过视觉引导的关键令牌正则化解决了MLLM遗忘学习中的关键令牌识别问题，实现了更有效的隐私保护遗忘，同时保持模型性能

Abstract: Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) prevents the model from revealing private information when queried about target images. Existing MLLM unlearning methods largely adopt approaches developed for LLMs. They treat all answer tokens uniformly, disregarding their varying importance in the unlearning process. Moreover, these methods focus exclusively on the language modality, disregarding visual cues that indicate key tokens in answers. In this paper, after formulating the problem of unlearning in multimodal question answering for MLLMs, we propose Visual-Guided Key-Token Regularization (ViKeR). We leverage irrelevant visual inputs to predict ideal post-unlearning token-level distributions and use these distributions to regularize the unlearning process, thereby prioritizing key tokens. Further, we define key tokens in unlearning via information entropy and discuss ViKeR's effectiveness through token-level gradient reweighting, which amplifies updates on key tokens. Experiments on MLLMU and CLEAR benchmarks demonstrate that our method effectively performs unlearning while mitigating forgetting and maintaining response coherence.

</details>


### [250] [From Logits to Latents: Contrastive Representation Shaping for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22028)
*Haoran Tang,Rajiv Khanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLReg是一种对比表示正则化方法，通过识别遗忘特征并将其与保留特征分离，减少遗忘-保留干扰，从而解决LLM遗忘中概念纠缠问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法主要通过预测空间的对齐目标来近似从头训练行为，虽然能减少遗忘内容生成，但可能只是抑制而非真正移除遗忘概念。遗忘概念可能在表示中持续存在并与保留知识纠缠，导致概念纠缠问题

Method: 提出CLReg（对比表示正则化器），识别遗忘特征并将其推离保留特征，明确减少遗忘-保留干扰，同时对保留特征进行最小化调整。该方法首次从理论上将表示塑造与纠缠减少联系起来

Result: 在不同规模的LLM和遗忘基准测试中，CLReg减少了遗忘-保留表示纠缠，促进了主流遗忘方法的有效性，且不增加额外的隐私风险

Conclusion: CLReg通过重塑表示空间来移除遗忘概念，为未来工作提供了新方向，表明表示空间的塑造对于真正移除遗忘概念至关重要

Abstract: Most LLM unlearning methods aim to approximate retrain-from-scratch behaviors with minimal distribution shift, often via alignment-style objectives defined in the prediction space. While effective at reducing forgotten content generation, such approaches may act as suppression: forgotten concepts can persist in representations and remain entangled with retained knowledge. We introduce CLReg, a contrastive representation regularizer that identifies forget features while pushing them away from retain features, explicitly reducing forget-retain interference with minimal shifts on retain features. We provide first theoretical insights that relate representation shaping to entanglement reduction. Across unlearning benchmarks and LLMs of different sizes, CLReg decreases forget-retain representation entanglement that facilitates mainstream unlearning methods without positing extra privacy risks, inspiring future work that reshapes the representation space to remove forget concepts.

</details>


### [251] [The Ensemble Inverse Problem: Applications and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22029)
*Zhengyan Huan,Camila Pazos,Martin Klassen,Vincent Croft,Pierre-Hugues Beauchemin,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种称为集成逆问题的新统计问题，通过条件生成模型构建后验采样器，利用观测集的集成信息进行后验建模，避免推理时显式使用前向模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高能物理中的反卷积问题、全波形反演和未知先验的逆成像问题，这些都需要从受探测器效应扭曲的测量中重建真实物理分布

Method: 提出非迭代推理时间方法，基于新的条件生成模型类别构建后验采样器，利用观测集的集成信息，通过跨多个真实-观测对集合的训练隐式编码似然模型

Result: 在逆成像、高能物理和全波形反演的合成和真实数据集上进行基准测试，证明方法能帮助后验推理并泛化到未见过的先验

Conclusion: 提出的集成逆生成模型能有效解决集成逆问题，避免推理时显式使用前向模型，利用集成信息改善后验推理并实现先验泛化

Abstract: We introduce a new multivariate statistical problem that we refer to as the Ensemble Inverse Problem (EIP). The aim of EIP is to invert for an ensemble that is distributed according to the pushforward of a prior under a forward process. In high energy physics (HEP), this is related to a widely known problem called unfolding, which aims to reconstruct the true physics distribution of quantities, such as momentum and angle, from measurements that are distorted by detector effects. In recent applications, the EIP also arises in full waveform inversion (FWI) and inverse imaging with unknown priors. We propose non-iterative inference-time methods that construct posterior samplers based on a new class of conditional generative models, which we call ensemble inverse generative models. For the posterior modeling, these models additionally use the ensemble information contained in the observation set on top of single measurements. Unlike existing methods, our proposed methods avoid explicit and iterative use of the forward model at inference time via training across several sets of truth-observation pairs that are consistent with the same forward model, but originate from a wide range of priors. We demonstrate that this training procedure implicitly encodes the likelihood model. The use of ensemble information helps posterior inference and enables generalization to unseen priors. We benchmark the proposed method on several synthetic and real datasets in inverse imaging, HEP, and FWI. The codes are available at https://github.com/ZhengyanHuan/The-Ensemble-Inverse-Problem--Applications-and-Methods.

</details>


### [252] [Per-parameter Task Arithmetic for Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22030)
*Chengyi Cai,Zesheng Ye,Jiangchao Yao,Jianzhong Qi,Bo Han,Xiaolu Zhang,Feng Liu,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerTA：一种基于参数级任务算术的大语言模型遗忘方法，通过重新缩放任务向量来平衡遗忘与保留，避免过度遗忘


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务算术遗忘方法通过减去任务向量来移除私有信息，但会导致过度遗忘，破坏模型保留其他重要信息的能力。观察到不同参数在遗忘与保留中的重要性不同，需要参数级调整机制。

Method: 提出参数级任务算术（PerTA）机制，通过重新缩放任务向量实现参数级调整。使用两种方法估计权重：基于梯度的PerTA-grad和基于对角Fisher信息近似的PerTA-fisher。权重量化每个参数在遗忘与保留中的相对重要性。

Result: 实验表明PerTA相比标准任务向量持续改进，在许多情况下超越广泛使用的基于训练的遗忘方法，在遗忘效果和模型整体效用方面表现更优。保持任务算术效率的同时缓解了过度遗忘问题。

Conclusion: PerTA为大语言模型遗忘提供了一个原则性且实用的框架，通过参数级调整任务向量，在保持效率的同时平衡遗忘与保留，解决了传统任务算术的过度遗忘问题。

Abstract: In large language model (LLM) unlearning, private information is required to be removed. Task arithmetic unlearns by subtracting a specific task vector (TV)--defined as the parameter difference between a privacy-information-tuned model and the original model. While efficient, it can cause over-forgetting by disrupting parameters essential for retaining other information. Motivated by the observation that each parameter exhibits different importance for forgetting versus retention, we propose a per-parameter task arithmetic (PerTA) mechanism to rescale the TV, allowing per-parameter adjustment. These weights quantify the relative importance of each parameter for forgetting versus retention, estimated via gradients (i.e., PerTA-grad) or the diagonal Fisher information approximation (i.e., PerTA-fisher). Moreover, we discuss the effectiveness of PerTA, extend it to a more general form, and provide further analysis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PerTA consistently improves upon standard TV, and in many cases surpasses widely used training-based unlearning methods in both forgetting effectiveness and overall model utility. By retaining the efficiency of task arithmetic while mitigating over-forgetting, PerTA offers a principled and practical framework for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [253] [Holographic generative flows with AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22033)
*Ehsan Mirafzali,Sanjit Shashi,Sanya Murdeshwar,Edgar Shaghoulian,Daniele Venturi,Razvan Marinescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合反德西特/共形场论（AdS/CFT）全息原理与深度学习和传输理论的生成式机器学习框架，将数据从基础分布到学习分布的流动表示为AdS中标量场的体-边界映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子引力的全息原理（特别是AdS/CFT对应关系）为生成式建模开发新的物理可解释范式，将流匹配算法与AdS物理相结合，提供比传统物理无关方法更优的收敛性和质量。

Method: 通过AdS中标量场的体-边界映射来表示数据从基础分布到学习分布的流动，将流匹配算法与AdS物理相结合，在棋盘玩具数据集和MNIST数据集上进行验证。

Result: 与可比较的物理无关流匹配模型相比，该模型实现了更快和更高质量的收敛，提供了物理可解释的流匹配版本，并证明了AdS物理和几何在开发生成建模新范式中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将AdS/CFT全息原理应用于生成式机器学习，为流匹配算法提供了物理可解释的增强，并展示了AdS物理和几何在开发创新生成建模范式中的价值。

Abstract: We present a framework for generative machine learning that leverages the holographic principle of quantum gravity, or to be more precise its manifestation as the anti-de Sitter/conformal field theory (AdS/CFT) correspondence, with techniques for deep learning and transport theory. Our proposal is to represent the flow of data from a base distribution to some learned distribution using the bulk-to-boundary mapping of scalar fields in AdS. In the language of machine learning, we are representing and augmenting the flow-matching algorithm with AdS physics. Using a checkerboard toy dataset and MNIST, we find that our model achieves faster and higher quality convergence than comparable physics-free flow-matching models. Our method provides a physically interpretable version of flow matching. More broadly, it establishes the utility of AdS physics and geometry in the development of novel paradigms in generative modeling.

</details>


### [254] [Cross-Fusion Distance: A Novel Metric for Measuring Fusion and Separability Between Data Groups in Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22036)
*Xiaolong Zhang,Jianwei Zhang,Xubo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Fusion Distance (CFD)作为表示空间中数据组融合程度的新度量，能区分融合改变因素和融合保持因素，具有线性计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布距离度量混淆了融合改变因素（如几何位移）和融合保持因素（如全局缩放和采样引起的布局变化），导致无法准确反映数据组间的真实融合程度，特别是在领域偏移场景下。

Method: 引入Cross-Fusion Distance (CFD)，一种原则性度量，能隔离融合改变的几何因素，同时对融合保持的变化保持鲁棒。该方法具有线性计算复杂度，并通过理论分析其不变性和敏感性特性。

Result: 在受控合成实验中验证了CFD的不变性和敏感性特性；在真实世界数据集上，CFD比常用替代方法更接近下游泛化性能的退化程度。

Conclusion: CFD为表示学习提供了一个理论基础扎实且可解释的距离度量，能更准确地量化表示空间中数据组间的融合程度。

Abstract: Quantifying degrees of fusion and separability between data groups in representation space is a fundamental problem in representation learning, particularly under domain shift. A meaningful metric should capture fusion-altering factors like geometric displacement between representation groups, whose variations change the extent of fusion, while remaining invariant to fusion-preserving factors such as global scaling and sampling-induced layout changes, whose variations do not. Existing distributional distance metrics conflate these factors, leading to measures that are not informative of the true extent of fusion between data groups. We introduce Cross-Fusion Distance (CFD), a principled measure that isolates fusion-altering geometry while remaining robust to fusion-preserving variations, with linear computational complexity. We characterize the invariance and sensitivity properties of CFD theoretically and validate them in controlled synthetic experiments. For practical utility on real-world datasets with domain shift, CFD aligns more closely with downstream generalization degradation than commonly used alternatives. Overall, CFD provides a theoretically grounded and interpretable distance measure for representation learning.

</details>


### [255] [Where Do the Joules Go? Diagnosing Inference Energy Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22076)
*Jae-Won Chung,Ruofan Wu,Jeff J. Ma,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大规模测量生成式AI推理能耗，发现任务类型、GPU利用率等因素导致能耗差异达25-100倍，提出基于内存和利用率的能耗分析框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源已成为机器学习计算的关键资源，仅测量能耗和观察趋势是不够的，需要准确理解能耗差异的根本原因以实现优化

Method: 在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上进行大规模测量研究，涵盖46个模型、7个任务和1858种配置，分析推理时间和能耗

Result: 发现显著的能耗差异：LLM任务类型导致25倍差异，视频生成能耗可达图像的100倍以上，GPU利用率差异导致3-5倍能耗变化

Conclusion: 提出基于内存和利用率等潜在指标的能耗分析框架，该框架可扩展到每瓦吞吐量这一关键指标，为算法、软件和硬件层的优化提供指导

Abstract: Energy is now a critical ML computing resource. While measuring energy consumption and observing trends is a valuable first step, accurately understanding and diagnosing why those differences occur is crucial for optimization. To that end, we begin by presenting a large-scale measurement study of inference time and energy across the generative AI landscape with 46 models, 7 tasks, and 1,858 different configurations on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs. Our empirical findings span order-of-magnitude variations: LLM task type can lead to 25$\times$ energy differences, video generation sometimes consumes more than 100$\times$ the energy of images, and GPU utilization differences can result in 3--5$\times$ energy differences. Based on our observations, we present a framework for reasoning about the underlying mechanisms that govern time and energy consumption. The essence is that time and energy are determined by latent metrics like memory and utilization, which are in turn affected by various factors across the algorithm, software, and hardware layers. Our framework also extends directly to throughput per watt, a critical metric for power-constrained datacenters.

</details>


### [256] [Boosting CVaR Policy Optimization with Quantile Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22100)
*Yudong Luo,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种改进CVaR-PG算法样本效率的方法，通过引入期望分位数项来利用所有采样数据，在可验证风险规避行为的领域中显著优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件风险价值(CVaR)使用策略梯度(CVaR-PG)面临样本效率低下的挑战，因为它只关注尾部性能而忽略了大量采样轨迹

Method: 通过引入期望分位数项来增强CVaR，分位数优化允许动态规划公式化，从而利用所有采样数据提高样本效率，同时不改变CVaR目标

Result: 在具有可验证风险规避行为的领域中，该算法在马尔可夫策略类中显著优于CVaR-PG，并一致优于其他现有方法

Conclusion: 通过分位数优化增强CVaR-PG可以解决样本效率问题，同时保持CVaR的风险规避特性，为风险敏感强化学习提供了更有效的解决方案

Abstract: Optimizing Conditional Value-at-risk (CVaR) using policy gradient (a.k.a CVaR-PG) faces significant challenges of sample inefficiency. This inefficiency stems from the fact that it focuses on tail-end performance and overlooks many sampled trajectories. We address this problem by augmenting CVaR with an expected quantile term. Quantile optimization admits a dynamic programming formulation that leverages all sampled data, thus improves sample efficiency. This does not alter the CVaR objective since CVaR corresponds to the expectation of quantile over the tail. Empirical results in domains with verifiable risk-averse behavior show that our algorithm within the Markovian policy class substantially improves upon CVaR-PG and consistently outperforms other existing methods.

</details>


### [257] [Prior-Informed Flow Matching for Graph Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22107)
*Harvey Chen,Nicolas Zilberstein,Santiago Segarra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIFM是一种用于图重建的条件流模型，通过结合嵌入先验和连续时间流匹配来提升图重建的全局一致性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分观测重建图是一个关键挑战：经典嵌入方法缺乏全局一致性，而现代生成模型难以融入结构先验。需要一种能结合两者优势的方法。

Method: PIFM将基于嵌入的先验（如图子或GraphSAGE/node2vec）与连续时间流匹配相结合。首先使用先验基于局部信息形成邻接矩阵的初始估计，然后应用整流流匹配来细化估计，将其传输到干净图的真实分布并学习全局耦合。

Result: 在不同数据集上的实验表明，PIFM持续提升了经典嵌入方法的性能，在重建准确性方面超越了它们以及最先进的生成基线模型。

Conclusion: PIFM通过整合嵌入先验和流匹配，有效解决了图重建中局部信息与全局一致性之间的平衡问题，为图重建任务提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: We introduce Prior-Informed Flow Matching (PIFM), a conditional flow model for graph reconstruction. Reconstructing graphs from partial observations remains a key challenge; classical embedding methods often lack global consistency, while modern generative models struggle to incorporate structural priors. PIFM bridges this gap by integrating embedding-based priors with continuous-time flow matching. Grounded in a permutation equivariant version of the distortion-perception theory, our method first uses a prior, such as graphons or GraphSAGE/node2vec, to form an informed initial estimate of the adjacency matrix based on local information. It then applies rectified flow matching to refine this estimate, transporting it toward the true distribution of clean graphs and learning a global coupling. Experiments on different datasets demonstrate that PIFM consistently enhances classical embeddings, outperforming them and state-of-the-art generative baselines in reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [258] [Learning Hamiltonian Flow Maps: Mean Flow Consistency for Large-Timestep Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22123)
*Winfried Ripken,Michael Plainer,Gregor Lied,Thorben Frank,Oliver T. Unke,Stefan Chmiela,Frank Noé,Klaus Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出学习哈密顿流映射的框架，通过预测选定时间跨度内的平均相空间演化，实现超越经典积分器稳定性限制的大时间步长更新


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿系统长时间演化模拟受限于数值积分稳定性所需的小时间步长，需要克服这一限制以实现更高效的模拟

Method: 引入平均流一致性条件，学习哈密顿流映射，预测相空间在选定时间跨度内的平均演化，无需访问未来状态，避免昂贵的轨迹生成

Result: 方法在多种哈密顿系统中验证有效，特别改进了基于机器学习力场的分子动力学模拟，支持显著更大的积分时间步长

Conclusion: 该框架能够稳定地进行大时间步长更新，超越经典积分器的稳定性限制，可直接在广泛可用的无轨迹MLFF数据集上训练

Abstract: Simulating the long-time evolution of Hamiltonian systems is limited by the small timesteps required for stable numerical integration. To overcome this constraint, we introduce a framework to learn Hamiltonian Flow Maps by predicting the mean phase-space evolution over a chosen time span $Δt$, enabling stable large-timestep updates far beyond the stability limits of classical integrators. To this end, we impose a Mean Flow consistency condition for time-averaged Hamiltonian dynamics. Unlike prior approaches, this allows training on independent phase-space samples without access to future states, avoiding expensive trajectory generation. Validated across diverse Hamiltonian systems, our method in particular improves upon molecular dynamics simulations using machine-learned force fields (MLFF). Our models maintain comparable training and inference cost, but support significantly larger integration timesteps while trained directly on widely-available trajectory-free MLFF datasets.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [The augmented NLP bound for maximum-entropy remote sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20970)
*Gabriel Ponte,Marcia Fampa,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出最大熵远程采样问题(MERSP)的增强NLP上界方法，建立NLP界与谱界之间的支配关系，引入对角缩放技术提升上界计算效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决MERSP问题中现有上界方法(NLP界和谱界)的局限性，这些方法已有25年历史，需要更精确的上界计算方法

Method: 1. 建立NLP界与谱界之间的支配关系；2. 提出基于微妙凸松弛的增强NLP界；3. 推导秩亏协方差矩阵的上界；4. 引入使用正参数向量的对角缩放技术

Result: 1. 证明了增强NLP界在特定条件下严格支配普通NLP界；2. 扩展了秩亏协方差矩阵的上界计算能力；3. 数值实验显示新方法在基准实例上显著提升了上界计算效果

Conclusion: 提出的增强NLP界和对角缩放技术显著推进了MERSP上界计算的技术水平，解决了现有方法的局限性，为秩亏协方差矩阵提供了新的上界计算方法

Abstract: The maximum-entropy remote sampling problem (MERSP) is to select a subset of s random variables from a set of n random variables, so as to maximize the information concerning a set of target random variables that are not directly observable. We assume throughout that the set of all of these random variables follows a joint Gaussian distribution, and that we have the covariance matrix available. Finally, we measure information using Shannon's differential entropy.
  The main approach for exact solution of moderate-sized instances of MERSP has been branch-and-bound, and so previous work concentrated on upper bounds. Prior to our work, there were two upper-bounding methods for MERSP: the so-called NLP bound and the spectral bound, both introduced 25 years ago. We are able now to establish domination results between these two upper bounds. We propose an ``augmented NLP bound'' based on a subtle convex relaxation. We provide theoretical guarantees, giving sufficient conditions under which the augmented NLP bound strictly dominates the ordinary NLP bound. In addition, the augmented NLP formulation allows us to derive upper bounds for rank-deficient covariance matrices when they satisfy a technical condition. This is in contrast to the earlier work on the ordinary NLP bound that worked with only positive definite covariance matrices. Finally, we introduce a novel and very effective diagonal-scaling technique for MERSP, employing a positive vector of parameters. Numerical experiments on benchmark instances demonstrate the effectiveness of our approaches in advancing the state of the art for calculating upper bounds on MERSP.

</details>


### [260] [Thompson Sampling Algorithm for Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20973)
*Asaf Cohen,Ruolan He,Yuqiong Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有未知共同漂移的线性二次随机微分博弈，提出基于Thompson采样的算法，证明贝叶斯遗憾界为O(√TlogT)，且算法收敛到纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有未知共同漂移的线性二次随机微分博弈问题，其中多个竞争性玩家在状态动态包含未知参数的情况下进行决策，需要设计能够处理参数不确定性的学习算法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯方法，假设漂移矩阵具有高斯先验分布，使用滤波技术更新后验估计。提出基于Thompson采样的算法，具有动态回合长度，用于近似最优策略。

Result: 证明每个玩家的贝叶斯遗憾具有O(√TlogT)的误差界，与玩家数量无关。平均单位时间遗憾趋于零，且算法最终收敛到纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 提出的基于Thompson采样的算法能够有效解决具有未知共同漂移的线性二次随机微分博弈问题，在保证遗憾界的同时收敛到纳什均衡，为多智能体强化学习中的探索-利用权衡提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study a stochastic differential game with $N$ competitive players in a linear-quadratic framework with ergodic cost, where $d$-dimensional diffusion processes govern the state dynamics with an unknown common drift (matrix). Assuming a Gaussian prior on the drift, we use filtering techniques to update its posterior estimates. Based on these estimates, we propose a Thompson-sampling-based algorithm with dynamic episode lengths to approximate strategies. We show that the Bayesian regret for each player has an error bound of order $O(\sqrt{T\log(T)})$, where $T$ is the time-horizon, independent of the number of players. This implies that average regret per unit time goes to zero. Finally, we prove that the algorithm results in a Nash equilibrium.

</details>


### [261] [The dual-path fixing strategy and its application to the set-covering problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20977)
*Paulo Michel F. Yamagishi,Marcia Fampa,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种双路径固定策略，利用对偶算法求解混合整数非线性优化问题松弛，旨在比基于单一对偶可行解的固定策略更强大，同时比"强固定"更快


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分支定界框架中，对偶算法自然应用于求解松弛问题，研究如何利用对偶信息更有效地固定变量，以提升分支定界算法的整体性能

Method: 引入双路径固定策略，利用对偶算法求解混合整数非线性优化问题的松弛。该方法比基于单一对偶可行解的固定策略（如混合整数线性优化的标准约化成本固定）更强大，但比"强固定"快得多，所需时间不超过所利用的对偶算法本身

Result: 在文献中的混合整数线性优化集合覆盖实例上成功测试了该方法，使用对偶单纯形法求解连续松弛问题

Conclusion: 双路径固定策略能够有效利用对偶算法信息进行变量固定，在保持计算效率的同时提供比传统固定策略更强的固定能力，有助于提升分支定界算法的性能

Abstract: We introduce the dual-path fixing strategy to exploit dual algorithms for solving relaxations of mixed-integer nonlinear-optimization problems. Such dual algorithms are naturally applied in the context of branch-and-bound, and eventual impact on the success of branch-and-bound is our strong motivation. Our fixing strategy aims to be more powerful than the common strategy of fixing variables based on a single dual-feasible solution (e.g., standard reduced-cost fixing for mixed-integer linear optimization), but to be much faster than ``strong fixing'', essentially requiring no more time than that of the dual algorithm that we exploit. We have successfully tested our ideas on mixed-integer linear-optimization set-covering instances from the literature, in the context of the dual-simplex method applied to the continuous relaxations.

</details>


### [262] [Noisy Pairwise-Comparison Random Search for Smooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21166)
*Taha El Bakkali,Rayane Bouftini,Qiuyi Zhang,Omar Saadi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出NCRS方法，利用随机线搜索适应内在维度k≤d，在仅使用噪声成对比较的情况下最小化高维光滑非凸目标函数，获得优于传统零阶方法的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零阶方法受环境维度d限制，而许多高维问题具有低内在维度结构。本文旨在开发仅使用噪声成对比较（而非函数值）的直接搜索方法，利用内在维度降低复杂度。

Method: 提出Noisy-Comparison Random Search (NCRS)：基于随机线搜索的直接搜索方法，通过噪声成对比较估计梯度方向。在均匀边界oracle下，复杂度为O(k/(p²ε²))；在平局感知噪声模型下，提出多数投票变体，复杂度为O(k²/ε⁴)。

Result: 建立了非凸收敛保证：NCRS在均匀边界oracle下达到ε-平稳性，复杂度显式用内在维度k替代环境维度d；在平局感知噪声模型下，多数投票变体达到ε-平稳性，复杂度为O(k²/ε⁴)。

Conclusion: NCRS是首个利用内在维度降低噪声成对比较优化复杂度的直接搜索方法，为高维非凸优化提供了理论保证，特别适用于仅能获取相对比较信息的场景。

Abstract: We consider minimizing high-dimensional smooth nonconvex objectives using only noisy pairwise comparisons. Unlike classical zeroth-order methods limited by the ambient dimension $d$, we propose Noisy-Comparison Random Search (NCRS), a direct-search method that exploits random line search to adapt to the intrinsic dimension $k \le d$. We establish novel non-convex convergence guarantees for approximate stationarity: under a uniform-margin oracle, NCRS attains $ε$-stationarity with complexity $\mathcal{O}(k/(p^{2}ε^{2}))$, explicitly replacing ambient dependence with the intrinsic dimension. Furthermore, we introduce a general tie-aware noise model where comparison quality degrades near ties; for this setting, we prove that a majority-vote variant of NCRS achieves $ε$-stationarity with complexity $\mathcal{O}(k^{2}/ε^{4})$.

</details>


### [263] [Solving the Offline and Online Min-Max Problem of Non-smooth Submodular-Concave Functions: A Zeroth-Order Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21243)
*Amir Ali Farzin,Yuen-Man Pun,Philipp Braun,Tyler Summers,Iman Shames*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对非光滑、子模-凹函数的零阶鞍点算法，结合Lovász扩展和高斯平滑技术，在离线和在线场景下分别证明收敛性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有非光滑、子模（关于最小化变量）和凹（关于最大化变量）特性的max-min/min-max问题的求解方法。这类问题在组合优化、鲁棒优化等领域常见，但传统方法难以处理非光滑性和子模结构。

Method: 提出零阶鞍点算法：1) 对最小化变量使用目标函数Lovász扩展的次梯度；2) 对最大化变量使用高斯平滑技术估计平滑函数的梯度。算法在离线和在线两种设置下进行分析。

Result: 1) 离线情况下，算法在期望意义下收敛到ε-鞍点；2) 在线情况下，算法在期望意义下达到O(√(N·P̄_N))的在线对偶间隙，其中N为迭代次数，P̄_N为最优决策序列的路径长度。提供了复杂度分析和超参数选择。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理非光滑、子模-凹函数的鞍点问题，在离线和在线场景下均有理论保证。数值实验验证了理论结果，为这类复杂优化问题提供了可行的求解方案。

Abstract: We consider max-min and min-max problems with objective functions that are possibly non-smooth, submodular with respect to the minimiser and concave with respect to the maximiser. We investigate the performance of a zeroth-order method applied to this problem. The method is based on the subgradient of the Lovász extension of the objective function with respect to the minimiser and based on Gaussian smoothing to estimate the smoothed function gradient with respect to the maximiser. In expectation sense, we prove the convergence of the algorithm to an $ε$-saddle point in the offline case. Moreover, we show that, in the expectation sense, in the online setting, the algorithm achieves $O(\sqrt{N\bar{P}_N})$ online duality gap, where $N$ is the number of iterations and $\bar{P}_N$ is the path length of the sequence of optimal decisions. The complexity analysis and hyperparameter selection are presented for all the cases. The theoretical results are illustrated via numerical examples.

</details>


### [264] [Certifying optimality in nonconvex robust PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21333)
*Pinxi Gong,Lexiao Lai,Jianhao Ma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了鲁棒主成分分析中基于绝对值残差最小化的非光滑非凸优化问题，证明了在标准不相干条件和随机损坏模型下，真实低秩矩阵的因子分解解具有特定的临界点性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒主成分分析旨在从带有稀疏损坏的完全观测数据中恢复低秩矩阵。传统的基于绝对值残差最小化的方法会产生非光滑非凸目标函数，需要理解其优化景观以确保算法能够找到全局最优解。

Method: 研究采用因子分解方法，通过最小化逐项绝对残差来拟合低秩矩阵。在标准不相干条件和随机损坏支持模型下，分析真实秩r矩阵的因子分解解的性质，包括Clarke临界点、尖锐局部最小值和严格鞍点。

Result: 在高概率下，真实秩r矩阵的所有因子分解解都是Clarke临界点。当因子分解秩等于r时，这些解是尖锐局部最小值；当因子分解秩超过r时，它们是严格鞍点。

Conclusion: 该研究为鲁棒主成分分析的非光滑非凸优化问题提供了理论保证，表明在适当条件下，基于绝对值残差最小化的因子分解方法具有良好的优化景观，有助于设计有效的优化算法。

Abstract: Robust principal component analysis seeks to recover a low-rank matrix from fully observed data with sparse corruptions. A scalable approach fits a low-rank factorization by minimizing the sum of entrywise absolute residuals, leading to a nonsmooth and nonconvex objective. Under standard incoherence conditions and a random model for the corruption support, we study factorizations of the ground-truth rank-$r$ matrix with both factors of rank $r$. With high probability, every such factorization is a Clarke critical point. We also characterize the local geometry: when the factorization rank equals $r$, these solutions are sharp local minima; when it exceeds $r$, they are strict saddle points.

</details>


### [265] [Decentralized Learning with Dynamically Refined Edge Weights: A Data-Dependent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21355)
*Rongxing Du,Hoi-To Wai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出D3GD框架，通过数据依赖的动态有向图边权重设计加速去中心化优化，相比传统Di-DGD提升30-40%收敛速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有向图去中心化梯度下降(Di-DGD)算法在边权重设计上缺乏数据适应性，限制了优化收敛速度。需要开发能够根据数据特征动态调整通信策略的框架来加速去中心化优化

Method: 提出动态有向去中心化梯度(D3GD)框架，从Di-DGD的新收敛分析中推导出成本函数启发式设计函数，实现数据依赖的边权重动态设计。开发完全去中心化版本，每个智能体仅使用邻居信息优化通信策略

Result: 数值实验表明D3GD相比Di-DGD加速收敛30-40%，学习到的边权重能够适应数据相似性，为有向图上的去中心化学习提供了实用替代方案

Conclusion: D3GD框架通过数据依赖的边权重动态设计有效加速了去中心化优化，为有向图上的分布式学习提供了更高效的算法，边权重能够自适应数据特征

Abstract: This paper aims to accelerate decentralized optimization by strategically designing the edge weights used in the agent-to-agent message exchanges. We propose a Dynamic Directed Decentralized Gradient (D3GD) framework and show that the proposed data-dependent framework is a practical alternative to the classical directed DGD (Di-DGD) algorithm for learning on directed graphs. To obtain a strategy for edge weights refinement, we derive a design function inspired by the cost-to-go function in a new convergence analysis for Di-DGD. This results in a data-dependent dynamical design for the edge weights. A fully decentralized version of D3GD is developed such that each agent refines its communication strategy using only neighbor's information. Numerical experiments show that D3GD accelerates convergence towards stationary solution by 30-40\% over Di-DGD, and learns edge weights that adapt to data similarity.

</details>


### [266] [Manifold constrained steepest descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21487)
*Kaiwei Yang,Lexiao Lai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出MCSD框架，将范数约束线性最小化oracle扩展到流形约束优化问题，避免了传统方法中的嵌套循环，并通过投影实现单循环优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于范数约束线性最小化oracle的优化器在大规模学习中很有吸引力，但将其扩展到流形约束问题具有挑战性，通常会导致需要迭代求解切空间子问题的嵌套循环方案。

Method: 提出流形约束最速下降法（MCSD），通过将LMO应用于黎曼梯度来选择范数诱导的最速下降方向，然后通过投影返回到流形。进一步在Stiefel流形上引入谱范数特化的SPEL方法，利用快速矩阵符号计算实现可扩展实现。

Result: 在标准光滑性假设下，建立了MCSD及其随机动量变体的收敛保证。在PCA、正交约束CNN和流形约束LLM适配器调优实验中，相比标准黎曼基线和现有流形感知LMO方法，显示出改进的稳定性和有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: MCSD为流形约束优化提供了一个有效的单循环框架，避免了传统嵌套循环方案的复杂性，在多个实际应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Norm-constrained linear minimization oracle (LMO)-based optimizers such as spectral gradient descent and Muon are attractive in large-scale learning, but extending them to manifold-constrained problems is nontrivial and often leads to nested-loop schemes that solve tangent-space subproblems iteratively. We propose \emph{Manifold Constrained Steepest Descent} (MCSD), a single-loop framework for optimization over manifolds that selects a norm-induced steepest-descent direction via an LMO applied to the Riemannian gradient, and then returns to the manifold via projection. Under standard smoothness assumptions, we establish convergence guarantees for MCSD and a stochastic momentum variant. We further introduce \emph{SPEL}, the spectral-norm specialization of MCSD on the Stiefel manifold, which admits scalable implementations via fast matrix sign computations. Experiments on PCA, orthogonality-constrained CNNs, and manifold-constrained LLM adapter tuning demonstrate improved stability and competitive performance relative to standard Riemannian baselines and existing manifold-aware LMO methods.

</details>


### [267] [A data-based image representation for continuous-time LTI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21606)
*Amine Othmane,Philipp Schmitz,Karl Worthmann,Kathrin Flaßkamp*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种从数据中获取未知线性系统图像表示的数值稳定方法，基于连续时间基本引理，避免求解微分代数方程，使用代数微分器近似导数，显著降低数据驱动表示的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法需要求解微分代数方程，计算复杂且数值不稳定，需要一种更简单、更稳定的方法来从数据中获取线性系统的图像表示。

Method: 基于连续时间版本的Willems基本引理，提出数据驱动的图像表示方法，避免求解微分代数方程，使用代数微分器近似导数，消除冗余自由度，减少待识别未知量的数量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在严重测量干扰存在的情况下，所提方法仍然有效，验证了其数值稳定性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的图像表示方法为数据驱动的线性系统分析提供了一种数值稳定、复杂度低的替代方案，特别适用于存在测量噪声的实际应用场景。

Abstract: We derive a numerically stable method to obtain an image representation of an unknown linear system only from data, leveraging a continuous-time version of Willems et al.'s fundamental lemma. We propose a data-based representation that, unlike previous approaches, avoids solving differential-algebraic equations and uses derivatives approximated by algebraic differentiators. Our image-based formulation significantly reduces the complexity of the data-driven representation by eliminating redundant degrees of freedom and thus reducing the number of unknown quantities to be identified. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed approach, even in the presence of severe measurement disturbances.

</details>


### [268] [Outrunning the Omega Clock: A Singular Control Problem for Dividend Optimisation with Ruin and Time-in-Distress Default](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21705)
*Andi Bodnariu,Nils Engler,Neofytos Rodosthenous*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文扩展了经典股息问题，引入基于占用时间的违约机制：当盈余过程在困境阈值下停留过长时间时触发违约，而不仅仅是首次触及零。这更真实地反映了财务困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统股息问题中，破产仅发生在盈余首次达到零时。现实中，即使未达到零，长期处于低流动性或资本不足状态也可能触发监管干预或运营失败。需要更现实的财务困境模型。

Method: 建立具有不连续状态依赖贴现和终止的新型奇异随机控制问题。采用定制的顺序猜测-验证方法提供完整解析解。识别与双重破产结构不同参数机制对应的三类最优股息策略。

Result: 发现三种不同的最优股息策略类别。对于某些困境阈值，最优策略具有不连续的行动和非行动区域。与经典股息问题不同，困境阈值下占用时间诱导的更高有效贴现可能导致股息支付延迟而非提前。

Conclusion: 基于占用时间的违约标准提供了更现实的财务困境建模。所得控制问题可通过定制方法解析求解，揭示了与传统模型不同的最优策略特征，特别是延迟股息支付的可能性。

Abstract: This paper extends the classical dividend problem by incorporating a novel, path-dependent mechanism of firm default. In the traditional framework, ruin occurs when the surplus process first reaches zero. In contrast, default in our model may also arise when the surplus spends an excessive amount of time below a distress threshold, even without ever hitting zero. This occupation-time-based default criterion captures financial distress more realistically, as prolonged periods of low liquidity or capitalisation may trigger regulatory intervention or operational failure. The resulting optimisation problem is formulated as a new singular stochastic control problem with discontinuous state-dependent discounting and killing. We provide a complete analytical solution via a bespoke sequential guess-and-verify method and identify three distinct classes of optimal dividend strategies corresponding to different parameter regimes of the dual-ruin structure. Notably, for certain distress thresholds, the optimal policy features disconnected action and inaction regions. We further show that, unlike in the classical dividend problem, higher effective discounting induced by occupation time below a distress level can lead to delayed, rather than earlier, dividend payments.

</details>


### [269] [On Diagonalizable Systems with Random Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21710)
*Yuan Zhang,Yutong Han,Yuanqing Xia,Aming Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究结构化矩阵在Erdős-Rényi随机图模型下的结构可对角化概率分布，分析不同边密度区间的渐近行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构可对角化对多变量系统分析和设计很重要，但缺乏对其在随机图模型中概率分布的深入理解，需要分析不同密度区间的渐近行为

Method: 利用图论方法表征结构可对角化条件，基于有向Erdős-Rényi随机图模型，分析不同边密度区间（稠密、中等密度、极稀疏）的渐近概率分布

Result: 稠密图几乎总是结构可对角化；中等密度图得到紧致的上下界；极稀疏图概率趋近于0；数值模拟验证了理论结果

Conclusion: 揭示了结构可对角化在随机图模型中的概率分布规律，为结构化系统分析和优化提供了理论基础

Abstract: Diagonalizability plays an important role in the analysis and design of multivariable systems. A structured matrix is called structurally diagonalizable if almost all of its numerical realizations, obtained by assigning real values to its free entries, are diagonalizable. Structural diagonalizability is useful for the verification and optimization of various structural system properties. In this paper, we study the asymptotic probability distribution of structural diagonalizability for structured systems whose system matrices are represented by directed Erdős-Rényi random graphs. Leveraging a recently established graph-theoretic characterization of structural diagonalizability, we analyze the distribution of structurally diagonalizable graphs under different edge-density regimes. For dense graphs, we prove that the system is almost always structurally diagonalizable. For graphs of medium density, we derive tight upper and lower bounds on the asymptotic probability of structural diagonalizability. For extremely sparse graphs, we show that this probability approaches 0. The theoretical results are validated through extensive numerical simulations with varying numbers of vertices and connection probabilities.

</details>


### [270] [Stochastic Optimal Linear Quadratic Controls with A Recursive Cost Functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21748)
*Lin Li,Jiongmin Yong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有递归成本泛函的随机线性二次控制问题，涉及L¹空间中的BSDE，建立了合适的理论框架，并研究了开环和闭环可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有递归成本泛函的随机线性二次控制问题，这类问题涉及L¹空间中的BSDE，其适定性是一个微妙问题，需要建立合适的理论框架来正确表述相应的LQ问题。

Method: 采用合适的理论框架处理L¹空间中的BSDE，研究开环和闭环可解性，通过前向-后向随机微分方程和Riccati微分方程的可解性来表征。

Result: 建立了具有递归成本泛函的随机LQ控制问题的正确表述框架，证明了开环可解性等价于FBSDE的可解性，闭环可解性等价于Riccati微分方程的可解性。

Conclusion: 成功建立了具有递归成本泛函的随机LQ控制问题的理论框架，并给出了开环和闭环可解性的完整特征描述，为这类复杂控制问题的研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a stochastic linear quadratic (LQ, for short) control problem with a recursive cost functional. It involves BSDEs in $L^1$ whose well-posedness is a subtle issue. A suitable framework has been adopted so that the corresponding LQ problem is correctly formulated. Open-loop and closed-loop solvability of such an LQ problem have been investigated and characterized by the solvability of an FBSDE and that of Riccati differential equation.

</details>


### [271] [ZOBA: An Efficient Single-loop Zeroth-order Bilevel Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21836)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出ZOBA算法，首个用于双层优化的有限差分单循环方法，在零阶设置下通过延迟信息消除嵌套循环，比基于Hessian近似的方法更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双层优化方法需要计算超梯度的一阶信息，但在零阶设置（仅函数值可用）下无法应用。现有有限差分方法依赖昂贵的双循环算法且并行性差，需要更高效的单循环解决方案。

Method: 提出ZOBA算法：1) 基于延迟信息的有限差分超梯度近似，消除嵌套循环；2) 分析非凸设置下的收敛性；3) 进一步提出HF-ZOBA（无Hessian变体）以获得额外复杂度改进。

Result: 理论分析获得复杂度O(p(d+p)²ε⁻²)，优于基于Hessian近似的方法。数值实验在合成函数和对抗机器学习黑盒任务上验证了方法有效性，达到与SOTA相当的精度但计算时间更少。

Conclusion: ZOBA是首个有限差分单循环双层优化算法，在零阶设置下通过延迟信息消除嵌套循环，理论复杂度更优，实验验证了计算效率优势。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization problems consist of minimizing a value function whose evaluation depends on the solution of an inner optimization problem. These problems are typically tackled using first-order methods that require computing the gradient of the value function ({\it the hypergradient}). In several practical settings, however, first-order information is unavailable ({\it zeroth-order setting}), rendering these methods inapplicable. Finite-difference methods provide an alternative by approximating hypergradients using function evaluations along a set of directions. Nevertheless, such surrogates are notoriously expensive, and existing finite-difference bilevel methods rely on two-loop algorithms that are poorly parallelizable. In this work, we propose ZOBA, the first finite-difference single-loop algorithm for bilevel optimization. Our method leverages finite-difference hypergradient approximations based on delayed information to eliminate the need for nested loops. We analyze the proposed algorithm and establish convergence rates in the non-convex setting, achieving a complexity of $\mathcal{O}(p(d + p)^2\varepsilon^{-2})$, where $p$ and $d$ denote the dimension of inner and outer spaces respectively, which is better than prior approaches based on Hessian approximation. We further introduce and analyze HF-ZOBA, a Hessian-free variant that yields additional complexity improvements. Finally, we corroborate our findings with numerical experiments on synthetic functions and a real-world black-box task in adversarial machine learning. Our results show that our methods achieve accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art techniques while requiring less computation time.

</details>


### [272] [When to Match: A Cost-Balancing Principle for Dynamic Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21858)
*Jie Liu,Hailun Zhang,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于成本平衡原则的自适应匹配算法，在不确定到达情况下实现有界竞争比，优于贪婪和阈值策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配平台面临即时匹配（减少等待成本）与延迟匹配（利用市场厚度）的权衡困境。现有方法存在计算复杂、依赖分布假设、缺乏最坏情况性能保证等问题。

Method: 提出成本平衡原则：当累积等待成本达到瞬时匹配成本的校准比例时进行匹配。基于流体极限分析设计无需分布假设的自适应成本平衡算法。

Result: 在对抗性到达下，算法达到(1+√Γ)的竞争比，其中Γ量化规模经济，保证成本在离线最优解的常数倍内。贪婪和阈值策略在对抗场景下可能产生无界成本。

Conclusion: 建立了在线匹配中不确定性的基本代价下界（黄金比率），算法在实际应用中优于行业标准启发式方法，为多边匹配提供了理论保证的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Matching platforms, from ridesharing to food delivery to competitive gaming, face a fundamental operational dilemma: match agents immediately to minimize waiting costs, or delay to exploit the efficiency gains of thicker markets. Yet computing optimal policies is generally intractable, sophisticated algorithms often rely on restrictive distributional assumptions, and common heuristics lack worst-case performance guarantees. We formulate a versatile framework for multi-sided matching with general state-dependent cost structures and non-stationary arrival dynamics. Central to our approach is a cost-balancing principle: match when accumulated waiting cost reaches a calibrated proportion of instantaneous matching cost. This equilibrium condition emerges from fluid-limit analysis and motivates a simple, adaptive Cost-Balancing (CB) algorithm requiring no distributional assumptions. We prove that CB achieves a competitive ratio of $(1+\sqrtΓ)$ under adversarial arrivals, where $Γ$ quantifies economies of scale, guaranteeing cost within a constant factor of the offline optimum. In contrast, standard greedy and threshold policies can incur unbounded costs in adversarial scenarios. We further establish a universal lower bound of $(\sqrt{5}+1)/2$ (the golden ratio), quantifying the fundamental price of uncertainty in online matching. Experiments on game matchmaking and real-world food delivery data demonstrate practical effectiveness, with CB consistently outperforming industry-standard heuristics.

</details>


### [273] [Pathwise Learning of Stochastic Dynamical Systems with Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21860)
*Nicole Tianjiao Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于变分推断的神经路径估计方法，用于从噪声非线性观测中同时学习随机微分方程先验和推断后验路径测度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数据中重建和推断随机动力系统是逆问题和统计学习的基本任务。实际应用中数据通常通过噪声和非线性测量间接观测，挑战在于不仅要近似SDE系数，还要同时根据观测推断后验更新

Method: 首先推导求解与路径wise Zakai方程对应的滤波后验路径测度的随机控制问题；然后构建生成模型，通过受控扩散和相关Radon-Nikodym导数将先验路径测度映射到后验测度；通过对观测过程样本路径的摊销，控制通过学习噪声观测路径的嵌入来学习

Result: 在非线性动力系统上进行实验，证明模型能够学习多模态、混沌或高维系统

Conclusion: 提出的神经路径估计方法能够从噪声观测中同时学习未知先验SDE和控制，恢复给定观测样本路径的条件路径测度，并学习诱导相同路径测度的相关SDE

Abstract: The reconstruction and inference of stochastic dynamical systems from data is a fundamental task in inverse problems and statistical learning. While surrogate modeling advances computational methods to approximate these dynamics, standard approaches typically require high-fidelity training data. In many practical settings, the data are indirectly observed through noisy and nonlinear measurement. The challenge lies not only in approximating the coefficients of the SDEs, but in simultaneously inferring the posterior updates given the observations. In this work, we present a neural path estimation approach to solve stochastic dynamical systems based on variational inference. We first derive a stochastic control problem that solve filtering posterior path measure corresponding to a pathwise Zakai equation. We then construct a generative model that maps the prior path measure to posterior measure through the controlled diffusion and the associated Randon-Nykodym derivative. Through an amortization of sample paths of the observation process, the control is learned by an embedding of the noisy observation paths. Thus, we learn the unknown prior SDE and the control can recover the conditional path measure given the observation sample paths and we learn an associated SDE which induces the same path measure. In the end, we perform experiments on nonlinear dynamical systems, demonstrating the model's ability to learn multimodal, chaotic, or high dimensional systems.

</details>


### [274] [On Approximate Computation of Critical Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21917)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Georgina Hall*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明：即使对于非常简单的非凸函数类，近似计算临界点也是NP难问题。具体而言，如果存在多项式时间算法能输出梯度范数不超过2^n的点（当函数存在临界点时），则P=NP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战非凸优化领域的一个普遍信念：近似计算临界点通常是可处理的。研究者希望揭示即使在简单函数类中，临界点近似计算也存在根本性的计算困难。

Method: 采用计算复杂性理论的方法，通过构造性证明展示计算困难性。主要技术包括：1) 证明即使对于常数次数的多项式，近似临界点计算也是NP难的；2) 在多种结构假设下（如临界点存在性、唯一性保证、函数有下界等）证明硬度结果；3) 使用梯度范数或到临界点距离作为近似度量。

Result: 主要结果：1) 如果存在多项式时间算法能输出梯度范数不超过2^n的点（当函数存在临界点时），则P=NP；2) 即使在临界点存在性、唯一性保证，函数有下界等强结构假设下，近似计算临界点仍然是困难的；3) 这些结果与非凸优化中普遍认为近似临界点计算可处理的信念形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 论文结论表明：近似计算临界点的计算困难性是根本性的，即使在简单的非凸函数类中也是如此。这挑战了非凸优化领域的常见假设，并暗示需要重新评估许多非凸优化算法的理论基础和实际可行性。

Abstract: We show that computing even very coarse approximations of critical points is intractable for simple classes of nonconvex functions. More concretely, we prove that if there exists a polynomial-time algorithm that takes as input a polynomial in $n$ variables of constant degree (as low as three) and outputs a point whose gradient has Euclidean norm at most $2^n$ whenever the polynomial has a critical point, then P=NP. The algorithm is permitted to return an arbitrary point when no critical point exists. We also prove hardness results for approximate computation of critical points under additional structural assumptions, including settings in which existence and uniqueness of a critical point are guaranteed, the function is lower bounded, and approximation is measured in terms of distance to a critical point. Overall, our results stand in contrast to the commonly-held belief that, in nonconvex optimization, approximate computation of critical points is a tractable task.

</details>


### [275] [Batched First-Order Methods for Parallel LP Solving in MIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21990)
*Nicolas Blin,Stefano Gualandi,Christopher Maes,Andrea Lodi,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于GPU的批量一阶方法，用于并行求解多个线性规划问题，特别适用于混合整数规划中的强分支和边界收紧等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数规划技术（如强分支和边界收紧）需要求解大量相关的线性规划问题，传统方法在GPU上效率不高。需要开发能充分利用GPU并行计算能力的批量求解方法。

Method: 扩展原始-对偶混合梯度算法，采用矩阵-矩阵运算替代重复的矩阵-向量运算，实现多个线性规划问题的并行求解，充分利用GPU架构优势。

Result: 在GPU架构上获得显著计算优势，通过多个案例研究验证了方法的有效性，并确定了在不同计算环境下，一阶方法优于传统单纯形求解器的问题规模范围。

Conclusion: 这是整数规划算法设计中充分利用GPU能力的重要进展，主张将特定操作完全分配给GPU执行，而非在CPU上使用轻量级启发式方法，为GPU加速的整数规划算法开发指明了方向。

Abstract: We present a batched first-order method for solving multiple linear programs in parallel on GPUs. Our approach extends the primal-dual hybrid gradient algorithm to efficiently solve batches of related linear programming problems that arise in mixed-integer programming techniques such as strong branching and bound tightening. By leveraging matrix-matrix operations instead of repeated matrix-vector operations, we obtain significant computational advantages on GPU architectures. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on various case studies and identify the problem sizes where first-order methods outperform traditional simplex-based solvers depending on the computational environment one can use. This is a significant step for the design and development of integer programming algorithms tightly exploiting GPU capabilities where we argue that some specific operations should be allocated to GPUs and performed in full instead of using light-weight heuristic approaches on CPUs.

</details>


### [276] [Anderson Mixing in Bures Wasserstein Space of Gaussian Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22038)
*Vitalii Aksenov,Martin Eigel,Mathias Oster*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于高斯分布的黎曼安德森混合（RAM）方法，通过重用残差历史来加速固定点迭代，在Bures-Wasserstein流形上实现收敛，相比Picard迭代显著加速，性能与黎曼梯度下降和共轭梯度方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多统计任务（如采样或计算Wasserstein重心）可以重新表述为概率分布上算子的固定点问题。加速标准固定点迭代方案为设计这些问题的有效数值方法提供了新途径。虽然Wasserstein几何不是精确的黎曼几何，但可以定义有用的黎曼概念（如切空间、指数映射和平行移动），这启发了黎曼数值方法的适配。

Method: 开发并实现了高斯分布的黎曼安德森混合（RAM）方法。该方法重用残差历史来改进迭代复杂度，相比Picard方法，额外成本可以忽略。证明了Bures-Wasserstein流形上的某些开球满足RAM收敛的要求。

Result: 数值实验显示，与Picard迭代相比，RAM方法实现了显著加速，性能与黎曼梯度下降和共轭梯度方法相当。该方法在Bures-Wasserstein流形上收敛，且额外计算成本可忽略。

Conclusion: RAM方法为高斯分布上的固定点问题提供了一种有效的加速方案，通过利用Wasserstein几何的黎曼结构，实现了比标准Picard迭代更好的性能，为概率分布空间上的数值优化问题提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Various statistical tasks, including sampling or computing Wasserstein barycenters, can be reformulated as fixed-point problems for operators on probability distributions.
  Accelerating standard fixed-point iteration schemes provides a promising novel approach to the design of efficient numerical methods for these problems.
  The Wasserstein geometry on the space of probability measures, although not precisely Riemannian, allows us to define various useful Riemannian notions, such as tangent spaces, exponential maps and parallel transport, motivating the adaptation of Riemannian numerical methods.
  We demonstrate this by developing and implementing the Riemannian Anderson Mixing (RAM) method for Gaussian distributions.
  The method reuses the history of the residuals and improves the iteration complexity, and we argue that the additional costs, compared to Picard method, are negligible.
  We show that certain open balls in the Bures-Wasserstein manifold satisfy the requirements for convergence of RAM.
  The numerical experiments show a significant acceleration compared to a Picard iteration, and performance on par with Riemannian Gradient Descent and Conjugate Gradient methods.

</details>


### [277] [An Invitation to Higher-Order Riemannian Optimization: Optimal and Implementable Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22126)
*David Huckleberry Gutman,George Lobo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在黎曼流形上寻找非凸光滑目标函数一阶和二阶驻点的首个最优p阶方法(p≥1)，建立了黎曼流形上非凸优化的最优oracle复杂度与欧氏空间匹配，并引入了研究黎曼流形上高阶正则性的系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黎曼流形上非凸优化的最优算法复杂度，填补现有研究在非凸黎曼优化中高阶方法理论分析的空白，建立流形优化与欧氏空间优化的复杂度等价关系。

Method: 提出基于回缩的高阶正则性分析框架，利用拉回连接和Sasaki度量研究目标函数的回缩拉回；推导新的协变Faà di Bruno公式获得简洁导数界；对于p=3的情况，通过新的基于Krylov子空间的框架最小化四次正则化三次多项式。

Result: 首次证明了黎曼流形上非凸优化的最优oracle复杂度与欧氏空间匹配；提出了首个p阶(p≥1)最优率方法；建立了高阶正则性分析的系统框架；对于三阶情况，开发了可实现的基于Krylov子空间的算法框架。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了黎曼流形上非凸优化的最优复杂度理论，提供了系统的高阶正则性分析工具，并为高阶黎曼优化算法的发展奠定了理论基础，特别是三阶方法的实现框架具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: This paper presents the first optimal-rate $p$-th order methods with $p\geq 1$ for finding first and second-order stationary points of non-convex smooth objective functions over Riemannian manifolds. In contrast to the geodesically convex setting, we definitively establish that the optimal oracle complexity of non-convex optimization over manifolds matches that over Euclidean space. In parallel with the complexity analysis, we introduce a general framework for systematically studying higher-order regularity on Riemannian manifolds that characterizes its joint dependence on the objective function and the chosen retraction. To the best of our knowledge, this framework constitutes the first known application in optimization of pullback connections and the Sasaki metric to the study of retraction-based pullbacks of the objective function. We provide clean derivative bounds based on a new covariant Faà di Bruno formula derived within our framework. For $p=3$, our methods are fully implementable via a new Krylov-based framework for minimizing quartically regularized cubic polynomials. This is the first Krylov method for this class of polynomials and may be of independent interest beyond Riemannian optimization.

</details>
